Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the automation framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 650. The resulting operator achieves bound O(935/n^5).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 650 + O(1/n^935). Summing over all modes and applying the automation convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
